### [magento2-module-chili-headless](https://github.com/enobis-GmbH/magento2-module-chili-headless)

Add chili headless functionality to Magento2. Allows common chili tasks via REST web api, e.g. create chili document, create document and directly consume outputs as pdf.

**Current version:** `0.3.3` (released 2025-02-07 14:17)
